Marvel's Midnight Suns

Spoiler-free 100% completion: discoveries, relationships, Abbey systems, combat records, balance rewards, campaign, and DLC · 25 areas · 1448 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead — it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Open the spoiler-free interactive guide

Completion Run Planner

You are here when: Use this checklist at the Mirror Table before advancing the main objective and again before starting New Game+.

The campaign remains playable after its final mission. Most cleanup is safest on the original clear file.

First-Campaign Foundation (missable)

Run plan

HintTreat the first campaign as the permanent exploration and relationship file.
WhereBefore the final mission, finish every Grounds region, club chain, Haven, hero friendship, and facility upgrade that is available.
Full detailUse repeatable general missions to advance days without advancing the main objective. Complete all four Words of Power, permanent collections, fifteen Havens, fifteen club meetings, base and installed-DLC friendships, every research project, and every purchasable Abbey upgrade in this same campaign. The final mission does not lock the save, so unfinished grinding can continue afterward.

One-Side Balance Run (missable)

Run plan

HintCommit Hunter's answers and deck to one alignment before changing direction.
WhereReach 200 Light or 200 Dark first and verify every reward on that side before reversing course.
Full detailChoose dialogue answers marked for the selected side and play four matching Hunter cards per mission for one point, or eight for two. The T.H.R.E.A.T. Room can add another same-day combat opportunity; the Grotto grants Light on a full moon and Dark on a new moon. At 200, verify both collars, the legendary copies, and the suit before earning opposite points.

Opposite-Side Balance Cleanup

Run plan

HintPreviously earned alignment rewards remain available after the meter moves away.
WhereAfter reaching one 200-point extreme, reverse every repeatable source until Hunter reaches the opposite 200-point extreme.
Full detailKeep the original campaign open and swap to an eight-card deck dominated by the opposite alignment. Run general missions and the T.H.R.E.A.T. Room with Hunter, playing eight matching cards whenever possible, and use the correct moon phase at the Grotto. Dialogue is finite, but combat balance is repeatable. Do not start New Game+ merely for this grind unless you intentionally want its reset rules.

No Difficulty or Rank Grind

Verified scope

HintDo not add an unsupported campaign-setting requirement.
WhereChoose any combat difficulty; 100% completion has no separate difficulty clear, time rank, or score rank.
Full detailFinish the campaign once on any setting. Raising difficulty increases mission rewards but does not add a distinct completion record, ending, or unlock requirement. There is also only one campaign ending, already represented by Campaign Completion.

Hunter Balance Rewards

You are here when: Hunter's Hero screen shows the current Light-or-Dark value; new collars, cards, and suits appear automatically at thresholds.

Crossing back through neutral does not remove rewards already earned.

20 Light - Ivory Collar

Balance unlock

HintKeep leaning Light until the next automatic reward appears.
WhereReach 20 Light Balance to unlock Ivory Collar.
Full detailUse Light-marked dialogue choices, play four Light Hunter abilities for one post-mission point or eight for two, and use the Grotto on the matching moon phase. Open Hunter's Hero screen to confirm 20 Light, then verify Ivory Collar is available before reversing alignment.

60 Light - Paragon Collar

Balance unlock

HintKeep leaning Light until the next automatic reward appears.
WhereReach 60 Light Balance to unlock Paragon Collar.
Full detailUse Light-marked dialogue choices, play four Light Hunter abilities for one post-mission point or eight for two, and use the Grotto on the matching moon phase. Open Hunter's Hero screen to confirm 60 Light, then verify Paragon Collar is available before reversing alignment.

100 Light - Summon Charlie

Balance unlock

HintKeep leaning Light until the next automatic reward appears.
WhereReach 100 Light Balance to unlock one Summon Charlie card.
Full detailUse Light-marked dialogue choices, play four Light Hunter abilities for one post-mission point or eight for two, and use the Grotto on the matching moon phase. Open Hunter's Hero screen to confirm 100 Light, then verify one Summon Charlie card is available before reversing alignment.

150 Light - Light Suit

Balance unlock

HintKeep leaning Light until the next automatic reward appears.
WhereReach 150 Light Balance to unlock the Light Suit and its passive.
Full detailUse Light-marked dialogue choices, play four Light Hunter abilities for one post-mission point or eight for two, and use the Grotto on the matching moon phase. Open Hunter's Hero screen to confirm 150 Light, then verify the Light Suit and its passive is available before reversing alignment.

200 Light - Summon Charlie Copy

Balance unlock

HintKeep leaning Light until the next automatic reward appears.
WhereReach 200 Light Balance to unlock a second Summon Charlie card for upgrading.
Full detailUse Light-marked dialogue choices, play four Light Hunter abilities for one post-mission point or eight for two, and use the Grotto on the matching moon phase. Open Hunter's Hero screen to confirm 200 Light, then verify a second Summon Charlie card for upgrading is available before reversing alignment.

All Three Hunter Legendary Abilities (missable)

Ability unlock

HintOne Grounds mystery awards a legendary ability based on current Balance.
WhereComplete Hunter's Forge challenge for Bladestorm, then finish the Garden mystery while at least 20 points toward either side for the opposite-side legendary.
Full detailHunter's Forge challenge always grants Bladestorm. At the Garden mystery's reward check, 20 or more Light grants Annihilation, 20 or more Dark grants Summon Charlie, and a neutral meter grants another Bladestorm. The 100-point thresholds separately grant the same-side legendary, so all three named abilities can be owned in one campaign.

New Game+ Carry-Over

You are here when: The New Game+ option appears after Campaign Completion and asks which updated carry-over categories to enable.

Current patched versions provide category toggles; the new campaign still resets world and facility state.

Carry-Over Selection

Replay rule

HintRead every toggle instead of assuming the replay imports everything.
WhereAt New Game+ setup, choose separately whether to retain abilities, friendships, resources, and cosmetics.
Full detailUse an updated game version, select the completed save, and review all four carry-over categories. Enable Hero Abilities to retain cards, mods, collars, and passives; Friendship Levels for hero and team rewards; Resources for currencies and crafting materials; and Cosmetics for unlocked and purchased appearances.

Progress That Resets

Replay rule

HintWorld exploration and facility progress belong to the new campaign.
WhereExpect character levels, Abbey exploration, research progress, facility upgrades, and one-campaign counters to reset.
Full detailEven with every carry-over toggle enabled, rebuild the Abbey route, re-earn Words of Power, raise research and training levels, complete research projects, and purchase facility upgrades again. Collections, Havens, clubs, reagents, keys, gifts, and other campaign-world state should be treated as reset until verified in the new file.

Post-Campaign Cleanup First (missable)

Replay rule

HintThe finished campaign remains available for unrestricted general missions.
WhereComplete balance, upgrades, friendships, collections, and combat counters on the original save before beginning a replay.
Full detailAfter Campaign Completion, load the clear file and continue advancing days with general missions. Finish every single-campaign counter there, especially Abbey upgrades, all Havens, all club meetings, suit palettes, daily sparring partners, Charlie's consecutive petting streak, and both Balance extremes. Start New Game+ only after the checklist is clean.

Mystery

Shaw's Church

Mystery

HintAn unusual landmark on the Abbey grounds has more than one interaction.
WhereThe old church and study sit in Valley of the Winds.
Full detailOpen the study, Reveal the middle-desk papers, inspect the rear cave, then collect three stained-soil samples: the western Abbey river dead end and both small Stone Terrace caves. Return them, Reveal the marked hanging tree, and take its Moon Seal to Everflowing Glade.

Lilith's Garden

Mystery

HintAn unusual landmark on the Abbey grounds has more than one interaction.
WhereA Break wall inside the greenhouse garden hides a ladder into a stone underground room.

Standing Stones

Mystery

HintAn unusual landmark on the Abbey grounds has more than one interaction.
WhereThree tall stones form a circle east of Dreamer's Descent.
Full detailUse Reveal at the circle. The three symbol sequences vary by save: copy them from the Abbey chapel wall, Agatha's Cottage wall, and the underground garden wall, entering each sequence exactly to summon three legendary chests.

Faery Rings

Mystery

HintAn unusual landmark on the Abbey grounds has more than one interaction.
WhereThree mushroom rings marked by blue torches sit in the southwestern and western woods.
Full detailUse Reveal on all three rings. One lies near the first western altar route; the other two are beyond the southern Whispering Wood Break wall and Reveal bridge. The last activated ring produces the legendary chest.

Renewable Exploration

You are here when: Arcane chests and reagent patches distributed through already explored Abbey grounds.

These resources respawn or cycle, so the checklist tracks their systems rather than pretending each pickup is permanent.

Arcane Chest Network

Renewable chest

HintColored chests appear beside ruins, caves, statues, and solved mysteries.
WhereOpen every newly revealed chest while sweeping the grounds; previously opened chests can later refill.
Full detailUse Arcane Keys for locked chests until Arcane Knowledge makes lower-rarity chests free. Because chest contents respawn, a reopened chest is not a missed permanent collectible; prioritize unique mystery reward chests and spend spare keys on unopened high-rarity chests.

Wundagore Everbloom Route

Rare reagent

HintA rare purple flower appears near magically resonant landmarks on particular nights.
WhereOn a waxing-moon night, check the Library, major Blood Gates, the southwestern altar, Standing Stones, the far cottage, and the greenhouse garden.
Full detailCheck the moon phase on the map at night, then fast-travel between magical landmarks and collect each glowing Wundagore Everbloom. It is renewable rather than a finite collectible, so repeat this route on later waxing moons if recipes require more.

Regional Reagent Sweep

Reagent route

HintDifferent plant types favor different sections of the grounds and can return after time passes.
WhereAfter rain or a new night cycle, sweep tree bases, graveyards, caves, and garden edges in each unlocked region.
Full detailCollect every visible plant during normal exploration, but do not treat empty spawn points as misses. Reagents are crafting materials that repopulate; revisit after missions or weather changes and reserve rare types until their recipe books are unlocked.

Hero Friendship

You are here when: Daytime conversations, evening hangouts, Havens, gifts, compliments, and sparring around the Abbey.

Raise every friendship-enabled hero to level 5; Hulk uses research instead of friendship.

Friendship Level 5 - Blade

Hero bond

HintSpend time with this hero between missions and learn which activities and gifts they enjoy.
WhereUse conversations, compliments, evening hangouts, gifts, sparring, and one compatible Haven to raise Blade to Friendship Level 5.
Full detailPrioritize Blade whenever a hangout appears, pair a loved activity with a matching gift, and use one Haven for a doubled friendship visit. Level 5 unlocks the hero's Forge challenge; friendship continues into prestige levels afterward, so no permanent reward is lost by overshooting.

Abbey Clubs

You are here when: Club invitations arrive through Superlink and lead to the Library, southwestern altar, or workshop cave at night.

Each of the three clubs has five meetings. Accept invitations promptly and recheck old Superlink threads when a series appears stalled.

Book Club Meeting 1

Club meeting

HintCheck private Superlink threads after each operation; an older invitation may be waiting without a notification.
WhereReply that you are ready in the Book Club Superlink thread, then return from an operation and meet the host at the Library that night.
Full detailNo advance reading is required. Reply to the newest unanswered Book Club invitation, complete an operation, and follow the host to the Library. If nobody appears, clear other queued evening events, advance normal operations, and check the same message thread again; declining only postpones the meeting.

Forge Challenges

You are here when: The challenge portal inside the Forge after the Armory is built.

Each fixed-hand puzzle awards a legendary card and suit; full card orders are kept at level 3.

Hunter - Child of Darkness

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Hunter's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory from Forged in Hellfire; Hunter's puzzle is available immediately. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Charge the nearest left target toward the upper-left one; Charge the upper-left target to defeat it; push the survivor right through the rocks; defeat the lower-right target with Charge to take position; use the last Charge and then Whip to send the survivor into the red crystal; play Bladestorm on the new black crystal.

Blade - Bare Fangs

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Blade's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Blade. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Quick Strike the right card-holder, finish it with Relentless, then play Make 'em Bleed. Split Strike across the two survivors, put every Daywalker chain on the stronger one, Quick Strike the weak one, use Reaper on the bleeding survivor, and finish the spawned crystal with Glaive.

Captain America - Guardian

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Captain America's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Captain America. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Punch the right card-holder into a pillar, play The Best Defense, then aim Shield Bounce through three weak targets. Play Tactician, Punch the last weak target, and spend both Shield Bashes plus the remaining Punch on the elite. Use the acquired Shield Charge on the crystal.

Captain Marvel - Hero's Heart

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Captain Marvel's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Captain Marvel. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Play Knee Strike and Cosmic Ray on the target, then knock it into the red crystal with Fists of Radiance. Go Binary, KO the target with Quick Jab, play One Step Ahead, Regroup, and All In, then hit the crystal with Fists of Radiance and Rain of Blows. Use Supernova on the black crystal.

Doctor Strange - Strange Memories

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Doctor Strange's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Doctor Strange. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Use Bolt of Balthakk on card-holding targets to collect Blessing of Vishanti and Agamotto's Gaze. Alternate the Gaze draws with both Blessings, then direct every strengthened Bolt into the remaining target and crystal. When Seven Suns of Cinnibus appears, cast it on the black crystal.

Ghost Rider - Dark Heart

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Ghost Rider's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Ghost Rider. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Lash the second target into the third, then Lash the fourth into the second. Use Judgement on the clustered pair, Retribution the remaining weak target into rubble, and use Penance Stare on the elite; play the awarded card if the closing target remains.

Hulk - Monster or Man?

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Hulk's unlocked challenge.
Full detailResearch Change of Plans, complete three Hulk missions, upgrade three Hulk abilities, then research Energy Drink. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Gamma Kick the fourth target; follow with Smash and Crush to KO it. Chain Rampage through targets one, two, and three, Gamma Kick target five, play Let's Do This, then Smash once more to reach maximum Rage. Use Worldbreaker on the full group.

Iron Man - Iron Will

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Iron Man's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Iron Man. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Redraw New Plan, then play Heads Up, Precision, and Leave It to Me. Put the eight-hit Surgical Strike into the red crystal, play the next Heads Up, use the six-hit Surgical Strike, and fire all three Blasts at the crystal. Finish the spawned crystal with Hellfire Beam.

Magik - Demon Child

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Magik's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Magik. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Play Limbo's Grasp, place a Limbo Portal at the green hazard, and Quick Soul Slash the central target through it. Use Trap Door, detonate the red hazard, then alternate Kick, Trap Door, and Soul Blast to collide the surviving target with its neighbors. Play Darkchylde and end the turn.

Nico Minoru - Black Shadows

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Nico Minoru's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Nico Minoru. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Redraw Blood Magic for Double Up, play both Double Ups, then redraw Empower and cast the new Blood Magic on Nico. Put one Witchstorm into each pair of untouched targets and play both Witchfires to clean up. Use Crack the Sky when it appears.

Scarlet Witch - Witch's Trial

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Scarlet Witch's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Scarlet Witch. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Use both Quick Toss cards to pull the two distant targets into Wanda's radius. Cast Hex Field twice, then chain successive Hex Bolts through targets three, two, four, and one while collecting the replacement Bolts. Finish the elite with No More.

Spider-Man - Wall Crawler

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Spider-Man's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Spider-Man. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Play Opportunist. Use the low coffin to knock the elite into one weak target, then the left coffin into the second. Throw the last weak target into the elite with Special Delivery and use the remaining coffin to finish it. Play Infernal Spider, then empty the free follow-up cards into the crystal.

Wolverine - Bone & Claw

Hero challenge

HintA fixed-hand Forge puzzle rewards careful card order rather than a normal combat build.
WhereAt the Forge challenge portal, select Wolverine's unlocked challenge.
Full detailBuild the Armory, complete Hunter's challenge, and reach Friendship Level 5 with Wolverine. Number ordinary targets left-to-right before acting. Put every Lethal Pounce chain on the nearer right target for Strengthened. Split Chain Swipes across the two right targets, Power Slash each left target into the short wall, and use Stink of Fear on the left pair. Play Let's Do This, split Eviscerate two-and-two across the survivors, then use Midnight Special.

Optional DLC Completion

You are here when: Mission-table and Abbey activities that appear only when the matching add-on is installed.

This area is conditional; ignore it when playing the base game without the Season Pass heroes.

DLC hero bond

HintIf this add-on is installed, include its hero in the Abbey friendship rotation.

Morbius - Late Night Snack

DLC challenge

HintThis add-on hero has a fixed-hand Forge puzzle after maximum friendship.
WhereAt the Forge portal, choose Morbius's challenge after reaching Friendship Level 5.
Full detailBuild the Armory and finish Hunter's puzzle first. Number targets left-to-right before acting. Quick Claw the lowest-health card-holder, play Mist Form, then Claw the far-right card-holder and activate Bloodlust. Chain Shadow Strike once through all three survivors, Claw the left card-holder, chain the new Shadow Strike twice into the Feeding Frenzy holder and once into the other, then Claw and Feeding Frenzy. Storm - Make It Rain

DLC challenge

HintThis add-on hero has a fixed-hand Forge puzzle after maximum friendship.

Use Arc twice, concentrating chains until the front targets fall; after the distant Frenzy attacks, aim Vortex so both survivors hit their leylines. Destroy the crystal with Hellstorm.

DLC challenge

HintThis add-on hero has a fixed-hand Forge puzzle after maximum friendship.

Tendril Strike the two right targets, then put both chains of the second Tendril Strike into the same card-holder. Web Toss the far-left target into the leyline, Spike Burst the four injured targets, play Insatiable Hunger, and use Tasty Brains on the survivor. Activate Assimilation, Tasty Brains one new target, and Lethal Embrace the other.

Base Completion Records

You are here when: Track these through the corresponding Forge, Yard, War Room, Hero, customization, and combat menus.

These are game-internal completion actions and collection records.

Item Bench Craft

Completion requirement

HintTry the Forge's consumable workstation once.
WhereCraft one combat item at the Item Bench.
Full detailResearch and purchase Item Bench, interact with it in the Forge, choose any recipe whose ingredients are available, and confirm one crafted combat item enters inventory.

Forge Ability Craft

Completion requirement

HintBlueprints can create a chosen hero card directly.
WhereCraft one hero ability at the Forge.
Full detailUnlock Ability Crafting, open its Forge station, choose a hero and an affordable discovered ability, spend the required Blueprint and Essence, and collect the finished card.

Cauldron Recipe

Completion requirement

HintA Grounds workstation turns reagents into useful supplies.
WhereComplete one recipe at Agatha's Cauldron.
Full detailUnlock the Cauldron through Grounds progress, collect the displayed reagents, select any known recipe, and brew it once; reading a recipe book without brewing does not complete the action.

Hunter Room Upgrades

Completion requirement

HintThe bedroom has its own Gloss-funded customization list.
WherePurchase ten different upgrades for Hunter's room.
Full detailInteract with ten separate furniture or decor positions in Hunter's room and buy a distinct option at each. Color variants at one position do not replace ten different room fixtures.

Every Research Project (missable)

Completion requirement

HintKeep studying Artifacts even after the essential facilities are open.
WhereComplete every available base-game and installed-DLC project at the Forge.
Full detailRaise Research Level by studying Artifacts, inspect each locked project's champion requirement, and deploy or upgrade the named hero until it is met. Research one project per day, claim it the following morning, and continue until every base tab and each installed DLC portrait has a completed mark. Purchase the upgrades separately afterward.

Every Abbey Facility Upgrade (missable)

Completion requirement

HintResearch alone does not build a facility improvement.
WhereBuild every Forge, Yard, and War Room upgrade in one campaign.
Full detailStudy Artifacts, finish each prerequisite, claim its research, and spend Credits on the resulting construction. Verify Forge: Item Bench I-III, Foundry I-II, Book of the Vishanti, Armory, Articulated Arm, Empowered Anvil, and Cauldron of the Cosmos. Verify Yard: Combat Therapy, Crucible, Legendary Blade, Teamwork Tactics I-II, T.H.R.E.A.T. Room, Training Dummies: Shove, and Training Dummies: Cure. Verify War Room: S.H.I.E.L.D. Exchange, S.T.R.I.K.E. Terminal, and Kree Processor. With DLC installed, also build Food Truck, Whisper Web, Laboratory I-II, and Enhanced T.H.R.E.A.T. Room I-II before New Game+.

Maximum Team Friendship (missable)

Completion requirement

HintGroup friendship grows through individual bonds and shared activities.
WhereRaise Team Friendship to its maximum level in one campaign.
Full detailEarn Friendship XP through Hangouts, Havens, gifts, Compliments, club meetings, and dialogue. Claim each Team Friendship reward at the central level-up display and continue until the team meter shows its maximum.

Twenty-Five Arcane Chests

Completion requirement

HintRespawning chests make this a cumulative opening counter.
WhereOpen Arcane Chests twenty-five times.
Full detailRaise Arcane Knowledge through exploration so lower-rarity chests become free, revisit opened chest locations after they reset, and perform twenty-five opening interactions. Unique chest locations are not required.

Every Hero Daily Sparring (missable)

Completion requirement

HintRotate the Yard's daily partner instead of repeating favorites.
WhereComplete Daily Sparring once with every available hero.
Full detailEach day, use Daily Sparring at the Yard with a hero not yet checked. Track the full base roster and installed DLC roster manually; only one partner is available per day, so advance with general missions and finish before New Game+.

Charlie Fifteen-Day Streak (missable)

Completion requirement

HintA daily Abbey interaction must remain unbroken.
WherePet Charlie on fifteen consecutive in-game days.
Full detailFind Charlie after every wake-up and interact until the pet animation completes before starting the next mission. Keep a manual day count; missing one day resets the streak, while post-campaign general missions provide unlimited recovery time.

Ebony on Four Days

Completion requirement

HintA second Abbey animal appears only on some nights.
WherePet Ebony on four different in-game days.
Full detailListen for the arrival cue and inspect the Abbey interior, chapel, library, and nearby common areas at night. Pet Ebony whenever found and repeat on four separate days; duplicate interactions on one day count once.

Every Hunter Ability (missable)

Completion requirement

HintHunter's full card pool spans Power, Light, and Dark branches.
WhereAcquire at least one copy of every Hunter ability.
Full detailAnalyze Gamma Coils, craft missing discovered cards, finish Hunter's challenge, reach both Balance reward tracks, and use the Garden mystery reward strategically. Compare the Ability Crafting list against owned Hunter cards until every ability is represented.

Ten of Every Reagent

Completion requirement

HintDelay mass brewing until every ingredient stack reaches double digits.
WhereHold or collect ten of every Grounds reagent type.
Full detailSweep all Grounds regions by day and night, including moon-phase-only Wundagore Everbloom spawns, until the cumulative requirement for every reagent reaches ten. Avoid spending scarce stacks at the Cauldron until the record registers.

Every-Hero Suit Palette (missable)

Completion requirement

HintEach recruited hero needs one alternate palette actively applied.
WhereDuring one campaign, equip a nondefault combat-suit color on each hero.
Full detailOpen Customization for each available base and installed-DLC hero, select a combat suit, and apply any nondefault unlocked palette. Repeat for the full active roster before New Game+; merely unlocking a palette is insufficient.

Every-Hero Mission Lead (missable)

Completion requirement

HintThe first portrait at the Mirror Table rotates the mission leader.
WhereUse every hero as leader for at least one completed general mission.
Full detailAt the Mirror Table, choose general missions whose required leader has not yet been checked, complete the objective, and return to the Abbey. Repeat for every base and installed-DLC hero; story missions do not substitute reliably.

Every-Hero T.H.R.E.A.T. Survival (missable)

Completion requirement

HintThe training portal tracks heroes separately.
WhereSurvive all three T.H.R.E.A.T. Room turns with every hero.
Full detailUnlock the T.H.R.E.A.T. Room, enter with one unchecked hero per day, and survive through the third turn. Use Quick and chain-KO cards to maintain Card Plays, then repeat for every base and installed-DLC hero before New Game+.

Hunter Cosmetic Spending

Completion requirement

HintHunter's wardrobe provides a large Gloss sink.
WhereSpend a total of 1,500 Gloss on Hunter cosmetics.
Full detailBuy Hunter outfits, room clothes, face props, and palettes from Customization until the cumulative spend reaches 1,500 Gloss. Purchases for other heroes do not advance this total.

Quick Villain Finish

Completion requirement

HintSave a Quick attack for a named target's final health.
WhereKO a villain with a Quick ability.
Full detailLower the villain until a Quick card's displayed damage is lethal, then use that card for the KO. Quick's Card Play refund is irrelevant; the final hit must come from the Quick ability.

Twenty-Five Multi-Knockback KOs

Completion requirement

HintLine enemies up with one shove repeatedly.
WhereKO multiple enemies with one Knockback twenty-five times.
Full detailUse Forceful Knockback, directional knockbacks, or shove angles that send one target into another and KO at least two enemies from the same action. Repeat until the cumulative counter reaches twenty-five.

Seventy-Five Redraws

Completion requirement

HintUse spare redraws every mission.
WhereRedraw a total of seventy-five cards.
Full detailDuring combat, select unwanted cards and use Redraw whenever charges remain. Card effects that redraw also help only if the game records them; the safest method is the standard Redraw command across missions.

Eight Abilities in One Turn

Completion requirement

HintFree and Quick cards stretch the normal Card Play limit.
WherePlay eight hero abilities during one turn.
Full detailBuild a hand with Quick attacks that secure KOs, Free cards, Marked targets, and Card Play refunds. In one turn, resolve eight hero cards before ending the turn; moves, items, and environment attacks are not hero abilities.

Five Interrogations

Completion requirement

HintResearch enables post-KO questioning opportunities.
WhereInterrogate five enemies across missions.
Full detailComplete the Truth Serum research and install its War Room upgrade. When an Interrogate card appears after an eligible enemy is downed, spend a Card Play on it; repeat five times across missions.

Two Drop KOs in One Turn

Completion requirement

HintOpen ledges can remove two targets without raw damage.
WhereKnock two enemies into Drops during one turn.
Full detailOn a map with two open Drop edges, weaken targets if necessary, then use knockback cards, moves, or shoves to send two enemies over marked Drop boundaries before ending the same turn.

Two-Turn General Mission

Completion requirement

HintChoose a short objective and front-load damage.
WhereComplete any general mission in two turns or fewer.
Full detailLower difficulty if needed, bring upgraded burst decks and combat items, and select a simple general mission. Finish its actual objective by the end of turn two; surviving an additional required wave would exceed the limit.

Villain Hero-Combo Finish

Completion requirement

HintSave a Combo card for a named target.
WhereKO a villain with a Hero Combo.
Full detailBuild Friendship until Hero Combo cards appear, lower the villain below a Combo card's previewed damage, then spend the Heroism and use that Combo for the final hit.

Five Environmentals in One Turn

Completion requirement

HintBank Heroism and leave props intact for one crowded turn.
WhereUse five environmental attacks during one turn.
Full detailEnter a room with several usable props, build enough Heroism, and perform five separate environmental attacks before ending the turn. Reusing a prop is allowed only if it remains interactable.

Ten Mission Challenges

Completion requirement

HintBuy optional objectives before launching general missions.
WhereComplete ten purchased Mission Challenges.
Full detailUnlock the S.H.I.E.L.D. Exchange, buy a Challenge for a general mission, meet its condition, and finish the mission successfully. Repeat until ten challenge rewards are collected; failed conditions do not count.

Untouched General Mission

Completion requirement

HintDefense, Conceal, Resist, and fast objective play prevent health loss.
WhereFinish a general mission with no hero taking Health damage.
Full detailUse a low-difficulty general mission, conceal or protect targeted heroes, remove marked attackers first, and finish the objective before any hit penetrates Block or Resist. Health damage from any teammate invalidates the attempt.

Four KOs with One Environmental

Completion requirement

HintA single explosive or heavy prop needs a tightly packed group.
WhereKO four enemies with one environmental attack.
Full detailOn a reinforcement-heavy map, weaken four minions near one explosive, pole, or large environmental. Confirm all four lie inside the preview and trigger that one environmental for all four KOs.

Three Legendary Abilities in One Mission

Completion requirement

HintBring three heroes whose legendary cards are already unlocked.
WhereUse three different Legendary abilities during one mission.
Full detailEquip one Legendary card in each hero's deck, bring those three heroes together, draw and play all three named cards before completing the mission. Duplicate copies or a single hero's repeated card do not count as three different abilities.

Ten Ability Mods

Completion requirement

HintThe Yard can add a random modifier to upgraded cards.
WhereApply mods to ten different hero abilities.
Full detailResearch and build the Crucible, upgrade ten distinct ability cards, then use Apply Ability Mods at the Yard on each. Re-rolling one card repeatedly does not replace ten different abilities.

Ten-Heroism Ability

Completion requirement

HintOne high-cost ability can consume a full Heroism bank.
WhereSpend ten Heroism with a single ability.
Full detailEquip a scalable or ten-cost Heroic ability, build Heroism to at least ten, and play it when its displayed cost is ten. Several smaller cards totaling ten do not count.

Twenty-Five Combat Items

Completion requirement

HintUse consumables steadily instead of hoarding them.
WhereUse twenty-five combat items across missions.
Full detailEquip combat items before deployment and activate them during encounters until twenty-five total uses register. Craft or buy replacements; carrying an item through a mission without activating it does not count.

Installed DLC Completion Records

You are here when: These entries apply only when the corresponding character packs are installed and their opening missions appear at the Mirror Table.

Finish all four character packs on one campaign before the combined closing mission becomes available.

Deadpool Mission Chain

DLC requirement

HintFollow one installed character's purple missions to their own conclusion.
WhereComplete every Deadpool story mission.
Full detailSelect each Deadpool-marked mission whenever it appears, complete its objective, then advance days until the next entry unlocks. Continue until his chain reports complete.

Deadpool Research Set

DLC requirement

HintHis missions unlock several themed Forge projects.
WhereComplete every Deadpool research project and build its upgrade.
Full detailUse Deadpool often enough to satisfy each displayed champion requirement, raise Research Level as needed, complete every project bearing his portrait, and purchase the Food Truck upgrade those projects unlock.

Maximum En Fuego

DLC requirement

HintChain Deadpool KOs without letting incoming hits erase momentum.
WhereReach Deadpool's maximum En Fuego level during combat.
Full detailUse Deadpool to secure the required KOs for each En Fuego step while Taunting away from him, Concealing him, or removing his attackers. Continue in one encounter until the meter reaches its maximum.

Venom Mission Chain

DLC requirement

HintComplete the full purple mission sequence for the symbiote character.
WhereComplete every Venom story mission.
Full detailSelect and finish each Venom-marked mission, advance days when the next entry is not yet present, and continue until his character chain reports complete.

Venom Research Set

DLC requirement

HintHis completed missions and deployments unlock themed projects.
WhereComplete every Venom research project and build its upgrade.
Full detailMeet each displayed Venom usage requirement, claim every portrait-marked project at the Forge, and spend Credits to build the Whisper Web in the War Room.

Ravenous Empty and Refill

DLC requirement

HintVenom's meter must visit both extremes in one encounter.
WhereCompletely spend and then refill Ravenous during one encounter.
Full detailUse Venom abilities that consume Ravenous until the meter is empty, then secure enough Venom KOs or trigger his refill effects to return it to maximum before the encounter ends.

Five Whisper-Web Missions

DLC requirement

HintA War Room upgrade can alter general mission conditions.
WhereUse the Whisper Web to modify five general missions.
Full detailBuild the Whisper Web, interact with it before choosing a general mission, apply one offered modifier, and complete that mission. Repeat on five separate general missions.

Morbius Mission Chain

DLC requirement

HintComplete the full purple mission sequence for the laboratory character.
WhereComplete every Morbius story mission.
Full detailSelect and finish every Morbius-marked mission, advancing days as needed between entries, until his character chain reports complete.

Morbius Research Set

DLC requirement

HintHis deployments unlock projects and a stat laboratory.
WhereComplete every Morbius research project and build its upgrade.
Full detailMeet each displayed Morbius usage requirement, claim all of his Forge projects, and purchase Laboratory and its available improvement before leaving the campaign.

Five Laboratory Subjects

DLC requirement

HintThe Laboratory's daily treatment should rotate among heroes.
WhereApply a Laboratory stat modification to five different heroes.
Full detailBuild the Laboratory, select one hero and complete a stat treatment, then advance a day and choose a different hero. Repeat until five unique heroes have received a completed modification.

Full-Mission Bloodlust

DLC requirement

HintActivate Morbius's stance immediately and keep it from expiring.
WhereUse Bloodlust on turn one and retain it through the mission.
Full detailDraw or fetch Bloodlust on the first turn, play it before ending that turn, and refresh or preserve its duration with Morbius's card effects until the final objective completes. Letting the status disappear invalidates the attempt.

Bleed-Free Vampyre Mission

DLC requirement

HintPrioritize biting enemies and block their marked attacks.
WhereFinish a Vampyre mission without any hero receiving Bleed from a bite.
Full detailUse Resist, Block, Conceal, Taunt control, and fast KOs to prevent every Vampyre Bite from landing on Health. Finish the mission and verify no hero ever gained the bite-applied Bleed status.

Storm Mission Chain

DLC requirement

HintComplete the full purple mission sequence for the weather character.
WhereComplete every Storm story mission.
Full detailSelect and finish every Storm-marked mission, advancing days between entries when necessary, until her character chain reports complete.

Storm Research Set

DLC requirement

HintHer missions unlock projects and a training-room improvement.
WhereComplete every Storm research project and build its upgrade.
Full detailMeet each displayed Storm usage requirement, claim all portrait-marked Forge projects, and purchase the Enhanced T.H.R.E.A.T. Room improvement.

Three Next-Turn Storm Cards

DLC requirement

HintHold delayed Storm abilities until three bonuses are charged together.
WherePlay three Storm abilities with active Next Turn bonuses in one turn.
Full detailRetain three cards bearing Next Turn text for at least one full turn without playing them. On the following turn, confirm each bonus is lit and play all three before ending the turn.

Four Storm Stuns

DLC requirement

HintGroup targets and combine Storm's stun effects in one turn.
WhereStun four enemies with Storm abilities during one turn.
Full detailBring Storm cards that apply Stun directly or through area and knockback effects, gather four valid enemies, then apply Storm-sourced Stun to all four before the turn ends.

Four-Pack Closing Mission

DLC requirement

HintA final DLC operation appears only after all character chains are complete.
WhereComplete every Deadpool, Venom, Morbius, and Storm mission, then finish the combined closing mission.
Full detailOn one save with all four packs installed, finish each character's full purple mission chain. Advance a day, check the Mirror Table for the new combined mission, complete it, and return to the Abbey to register the DLC campaign closure.

Mission Table Safety Check

You are here when: The Mirror Table and the explicit on-screen lock warning shown before the closing operation.

The campaign presents one ending rather than branching endings; make a manual backup when the lock warning appears.

Campaign Completion

Completion

HintThe Mirror Table eventually displays a large lock warning before a long operation.
WhereWhen the explicit lock warning appears at the Mirror Table, stop before accepting and make a separate manual save.
Full detailThere is one campaign ending and no light/dark ending branch to collect. After the backup, accept the warned operation and finish its connected missions; after the credits, load the continuation state for remaining Abbey exploration and friendship cleanup.

HintKeep opening new coils and checking newly recruited heroes and crafted items.
WhereLocate Leave It to Me in the matching hero, combat-item, or collection section and reveal its entry.
Full detailUse the Collections screen to identify which hero, item bench, or encounter owns Leave It to Me. For a hero card, open Gamma Coils after missions with that hero until it appears, then keep or craft a copy; for an item or encounter card, craft or trigger its named source once. Confirm the entry is no longer hidden.

HintKeep opening new coils and checking newly recruited heroes and crafted items.
WhereLocate Winds of Watoomb in the matching hero, combat-item, or collection section and reveal its entry.
Full detailUse the Collections screen to identify which hero, item bench, or encounter owns Winds of Watoomb. For a hero card, open Gamma Coils after missions with that hero until it appears, then keep or craft a copy; for an item or encounter card, craft or trigger its named source once. Confirm the entry is no longer hidden.

Research Project Catalog

You are here when: The research terminal beside the Forge work area.

Meet each project's stated research-level and hero-operation gate, then spend one day completing it at the Forge.

A Balanced Diet

Research project

HintRevisit the Forge after hero operations and research-level increases.
WhereFind A Balanced Diet in the Forge research list and satisfy the prerequisite shown beside it.
Full detailAt the Forge terminal, highlight A Balanced Diet; complete its listed hero operation or research-level gate, start the project, advance one in-game day, and collect its upgrade. If it remains absent, finish the associated hero's next operation and check again the following morning.

Wardrobe and Appearance Catalog

You are here when: Hero customization menus, bedroom wardrobes, friendship rewards, and Abbey chests.

Premium entitlements that cannot be earned on an ordinary current save are intentionally excluded and recorded in the audit report.

Ancestral

Appearance unlock

HintKeep checking friendship rewards, Arcane Chests, and the wardrobe after major upgrades.
WhereOpen the relevant hero's customization screen and find the locked entry for Ancestral.
Full detailSelect Ancestral in the relevant wardrobe or customization list and read its displayed gate. Complete that friendship, chest, balance, challenge, or campaign condition, then purchase the entry with Gloss if a price remains. Confirm it can be equipped; paid-only entries are excluded from this checklist.

Gift Catalog

You are here when: The Abbey gift shop, Arcane Chests, and glowing pickup points around common rooms.

Acquire each named gift at least once; giving it away still counts after its collection entry has been seen.

A Stoic Companion

Gift

HintCheck the rotating gift shelf and small glowing objects after each day.
WhereAcquire one copy of A Stoic Companion from the Abbey's gift sources.
Full detailAfter the gift shop opens, inspect its rotating stock each day and buy A Stoic Companion when offered. Also open Arcane Chests and collect Abbey pickups, which supply gifts outside the shop rotation. Once its name has appeared in inventory, it may be given to any suitable hero without losing completion credit.
